- **read-guard**: Stop Claude Code from filling its context window with whole-file reads of huge files and log dumps. A PreToolUse hook that denies Read of files over ~800 lines / 80 KB without offset/limit, and `cat` / `type` / `Get-Content` of big files with nothing limiting the output (cd-aware), telling Claude to Grep and read a slice instead. Retrying the exact same call is allowed, so nothing is ever blocked for good. Use when the user hits usage limits fast, when claude-token-audit shows Read or Bash output dominating, or when they ask to make Claude Code cheaper. - **reader-psychology**: Teaches the cognitive science behind how busy readers actually process written communications. Use when diagnosing why communications fail to land, designing high-stakes messages for distracted audiences, auditing existing copy for cognitive overload, or training a team to write with the reader's brain in mind. ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/reader-psychology) · ★63)
- **reader-question-refresh**: Repeatable cycle that mines real reader questions from YouTube comments (+ cached Reddit), has Sarah, Marcus and Chloe each rewrite their 3 worst-performing CW posts around those questions, ships them in one batch and records a 28-day before/after read. Use when Brew says "run the reader question refresh", "writers fix their worst posts", or when the every-two-weeks cycle comes due. First run 2026-10-13. ([MikeBrew123/carnivore-weekly](https://github.com/MikeBrew123/carnivore-weekly/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reader-question-refresh))
- **reader-questions**: 与えられた文章を注意深い読者として読み，抱く疑問をログする（読者質問パス）．内容の精査や，内容の変更を伴う文章編集の前段に使う．「読者として疑問を出して」「読者質問パス」で使う． ([k0tokay/kono-main](https://github.com/k0tokay/kono-main/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/reader-questions) · 2 copies)
- **reader-recap**: Conversational briefing on your recent reading — what you finished, what you highlighted, and what you had to say about it ([readwiseio/readwise-skills](https://github.com/readwiseio/readwise-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reader-recap) · ★329 · 488 installs · 7 copies)
- **reader-reliability**: Stage 9 reader QA skill — audit whether a text actually works in the product, classify silent blanks and support gaps, generate support-matrix evidence, and verify reader behavior across supported modes. ([lyceum-quest/orchestrator](https://github.com/lyceum-quest/orchestrator/tree/HEAD/.pi/skills/reader-reliability) · ★2)
- **reader-response-writer**: Drafts a thoughtful, on-brand response to a reader letter, subscriber email, or audience complaint — matching the publication's voice while addressing the reader's specific concern or question. ([ur-grue/autopunk-media-skills](https://github.com/ur-grue/autopunk-media-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/newsletter/writing/reader-response-writer) · ★46)
- **reader-retention-gate**: Run the reader-comprehension and retention gate before promoting SAGA fiction to release. Use to check whether readers actually keep reading, or before a release. Triggers: "독자 이탈 체크", "리텐션 게이트", "이거 끝까지 읽혀?", "reader comprehension pass", "retention gate", "will readers drop off", "release gate", "proper-noun overload". Checks proper-noun density, proposition overload, exposition-before-stakes, and first-scene hook; optionally spawns reader-persona subagents; writes a gate artifact. ([baksohyeon/QFUDS](https://github.com/baksohyeon/QFUDS/tree/HEAD/tools/saga-fiction-studio/skills/reader-retention-gate))
- **reader-review**: Run a fresh two-stage reader review of user-facing documentation, resolve its findings, and bind the final reports to the committed document state. Use for documentation blocks and source release preparation; this is separate from technical Critic review and creates no PO gate. ([agent-pipe-shared/agent-pipeline](https://github.com/agent-pipe-shared/agent-pipeline/tree/HEAD/plugins/pipeline-core/skills/reader-review) · ★4)
- **reader-review**: 对完整故事做独立读者审阅，报告具体位置的困惑、期待落差与注意力变化；由完整链或短篇链按需派发，只依据受审正文。 ([RoadtoAGI/MUSE](https://github.com/RoadtoAGI/MUSE/tree/HEAD/skills/MUSE-writing/skills/reader-review) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **reader-review**: Read a blog post as a first-time reader would and find where the story breaks — the on-ramp gaps, the unexplained leaps, the undefined terms, the "wait, what is this built on?" moments that lose someone encountering the system cold. Use when the user says "review this post as a reader", "does this post make sense to someone new", "where would a reader get lost", "is the story sound", "does this follow", or "this throws the reader in the deep end". This is a NARRATIVE-COMPREHENSION pass — it judges whether the post carries the reader, not whether it's polished, on-voice, or correctly scoped. F… ([EarlBear/blog](https://github.com/EarlBear/blog/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reader-review))
- **reader-seat**: Create, rewrite, restructure, diagnose, or compare reader-ready internet-industry work documents while preserving facts, scope, evidence, uncertainty, and author voice. Use whenever the user wants a substantive document written or revised from the reader's perspective or made reader-friendly, including requests to reduce reading effort, help first-time or cross-functional readers understand without guessing, surface what readers need to know, judge, or do, or better match content to its audience. Also use for clearer, easier-to-scan, less AI-sounding, or more decision-ready news briefs, techn… ([BobbyYue/reader-seat](https://github.com/BobbyYue/reader-seat/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **reader-side**: Write and revise text from the actual reader's side, excluding author-side process, implementation, and local context that does not help the reader understand, decide, or act. Use for PR descriptions, review comments, issues, release notes, UI copy, errors, notifications, documentation, handoffs, reports, and email. Preserve material risks, constraints, uncertainty, evidence, and required actions. ([cannorin/reader-side](https://github.com/cannorin/reader-side/tree/HEAD/skills/reader-side))
- **reader-signal**: Weekly reader-signal note from the Growth & Reader Analyst. Small-n honesty is… ([refluster/ai-native-article](https://github.com/refluster/ai-native-article/tree/HEAD/workforce/skills/reader-signal) · ★1)
- **reader-sim**: Load to read a draft as a specified first-time reader and report what reading it felt like, moment by moment, instead of a craft critique. Pass the persona, the draft, and what this reader already knows. ([haowjy/creative-writing-skills](https://github.com/haowjy/creative-writing-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/claude/skills/reader-sim) · ★594 · 645 installs · 5 copies)
- **reader-sim**: Read as a specified first-time reader persona and report the felt experience. Use for skill-only workflows when a draft needs persona-bound reader-response signal instead of analytical critique. - **reader-sim**: Симуляция первого читателя. Отчёт о реакциях по ходу чтения — где стало скучно, где перечитал абзац, где потерял, кто говорит, где предугадал поворот, где бросил бы.
